## Notes

- `CANONICAL` is assigned only to tracked files in canonical-looking locations such as `ogz-meta/specs/`, top-level guardrail/ops docs, or master alignment docs.
- `DRAFT` is used for inbox/proposal/ledger intake and untracked files in canonical-looking locations.
- `FOSSIL` is used for archived, backup, historical cognition, or session snapshots that may still be valuable but should not be treated as current doctrine without curation.
- `UNCLEAR+...` requires Trey/Codex ruling before treating the file as current source of truth.
